1. Smooth max projected gradient method on simplex.

Let's design practical loop:
```
L = next_power_of_two(2*n-1)
b_len = 2*n-1
b_target = np.zeros(L); b_target[:b_len]=1/b_len
B_target = np.fft.rfft(b_target, L)  # real? b_target not symmetric so rfft complex? np.fft.rfft of real returns complex for half.
# A_target_phase from sqrt(B_target)? But B_target complex.
A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
for iter:
    B = A*A
    # avoid zeros: ratio = B_target / (B + eps), maybe real? B complex.
    # update A = A * ratio**(beta/4)
    # But complex power. Use log: log_ratio = np.log(B_target + 1j*eps) - np.log(B+1j*eps); A *= np.exp(0.25*beta*log_ratio). This may have branch issues.
    a = irfft(A, L)[:n]
    a = clip(a,0,None)
    if sum<eps: break
    a /= sum
```
But if B has zeros or negative real? B is square of A, so its phase is 2*phase(A). B_target phase may not be reachable; ratio power adjusts phase and magnitude.

Alternative use time-domain b update: Compute b=irfft(A*A). Real. Then form b_new = b_target^α * b^{1-α} (geometric mean) over active? Then get A_new = sqrt(fft(b_new))? But need A_new consistent (phase of A). We can set A_new = sqrt(fft(b_new)) with branch preserving previous A? Might be simpler:
- b = clip(irfft(A*A),0) over active.
- b_desired = uniform.
- b_next = b_target**alpha * b**(1-alpha) (geometric mean) maybe.
- Compute A_next = sqrt(fft(b_next)) with branch such that phase continuous with A? Taking principal sqrt may flip. We can choose sign/phase to minimize difference to previous A: for each frequency, multiply by ±1 to maximize real dot? But rfft has independent Nyquist real. Since B_next must equal A_next^2; A_next = sqrt(B_next) up to sign per frequency, but Hermitian sign must be conjugate. We can choose signs to align A_next with A: for each k>0, if Re(A_next[k]*conj(A[k]))<0, flip sign. This yields stable phase.
- inverse, clip, normalize.

The spectral initialization may give better starts than random. Let's implement spectral initial function:
```
def spectral_initial(n, device=None, n_iter=20, alpha=0.5):
    L = next_pow2(2*n-1)
    b_len=2*n-1
    bt=np.zeros(L); bt[:b_len]=1.0/b_len
    Bt=fft(bt)
    # choose initial A as sqrt(Bt)
    A = np.sqrt(Bt)  # complex principal
    # fix Hermitian? if using full fft, ensure A = (A + conj(A[::-1]))/2? Not exactly.
    for _ in range(n_iter):
        b = ifft(A*A).real[:b_len]
        b = np.clip(b, 0, None)
        s=b.sum(); if small: break
        # flatten b: geometric mean with uniform
        c=1.0/b_len
        # maybe b_flat = c * (b/s? )? b sum not 1? Normalize b to sum1 first: b/=s.
        ratio = b/c
        b2 = c * (ratio ** alpha)  # alpha 0.5 reduces peaks
        b2 /= b2.sum()
        B2=fft(b2)
        # We need A2 such that A2^2 = B2. Take A2 = sqrt(B2). Choose signs to align with A.
        A2 = np.sqrt(B2)
        # For full complex, enforce alignment frequency-wise:
        # For each k, if A2[k] * conj(A[k]) has negative real, A2[k]=-A2[k]. But must preserve Hermitian. Instead use rfft half to set Nyquist, then rebuild? Simpler full with pair alignment.
        # Use A2_new = A2; for k=1..L/2: if real(A2[k]*conj(A2[-k])) <0? Already Hermitian if B real? B2 from real b2 so B2 Hermitian, principal sqrt may break. We can symmetrize: A2 = (A2 + np.conj(np.roll(A2, ...?)))/2? For DFT Hermitian: A2[-k] should = conj(A2[k]). We can set for k=0, L/2 (if even) real, and for k=1..L/2-1: A2[L-k]=conj(A2[k]). This loses info in negative half but consistent.
        # To align phase with previous A, for k positive choose sign so Re(A2[k]*conj(A[k]))>=0; then set A2[-k]=conj(A2[k]).
        # Nyquist if L even: make real; sign align with A[L//2].
        # Rebuild.
        a = ifft(A2).real[:n]
        a = np.clip(a,0,None)
        s=a.sum(); if s small: break
        a /= s
        # Update A = fft(a) for sign alignment next
        A = fft(a)
    return a[:,: maybe length n]
```

```
def correlate_valid(a, q, Lg=None):
    if Lg is None: Lg=next_pow2(3*len(a))
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,Lg); Q=np.fft.rfft(q,Lg)
    C=np.fft.irfft(A*Q, real_len=Lg)
    return C[1:1+len(a)]
```

Thus helper:
```
def correlate_weights(a, q, Lg):
    n=len(a)
    qfull=np.zeros(Lg); qfull[:len(q)]=q
    rq=qfull[::-1]
    C=np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(a,Lg)*np.fft.rfft(rq,Lg), Lg)
    idx = Lg - 1 - np.arange(n)
    return C[idx]
```
Need ensure no wrap: Lg >= n + len(q) -1. Good.

For b convolution, use Lb=next_pow2(2n-1). Can compute C=irfft(rfft(a)*rfft(a)). Good.

def polish_sequence(a, n, Lb, Lg, idx, deadline, initial_alpha=0.1, min_tau_factor=0.005):
    theta = log(a+eps)
    theta -= a.mean()? subtract theta.mean for stability
    best_a=a.copy(); best_local=score_internal(a)
    no_improve=0
    alpha=initial_alpha
    tau_factor=0.05
    while time < deadline:
        a=softmax(theta)
        b=conv
        mb=max(b)
        current=2*n*mb
        # update best local maybe
        if current < best_local: best_local=current; best_local_a=a.copy(); no_improve=0; alpha=min(alpha*1.5?) else no_improve++
        # compute q
        tau = max(1e-300, mb / (1.0/tau_factor))? = mb / tau_factor? If tau_factor=0.05, tau=mb/0.05 =20mb. Use `tau = mb / sharpness`, sharpness maybe 20.
        x=(b - mb)/tau
        x -= x.max()
        q=exp(x); q/=q.sum()
        c=correlate_weights(a,q,Lg,idx)
        g=4.0*n*c  # gradient of score wrt a
        h = g - np.dot(a,g)
        if max_h>small:
            h_norm = h / max_h
        else break
        # Try alphas decreasing
        for alpha_try in alpha_sequence:
            theta_try = theta - alpha_try * h_norm
            # maybe remove mean: theta_try -= theta_try.mean()
            a_try = softmax(theta_try)
            b_try=conv; mb_try=max; score_try=2*n*mb_try
            if score_try < current - tolerance:
                 theta=theta_try; alpha=alpha_try? accept; break
        else:
            alpha = alpha*0.5; if alpha too small break.
```

def spectral_iterate(a, n, L, rng, iters=500, flatten_alpha=0.7, perturb_prob=0.1):
    A = rfft(a, L)
    b_len=2n-1; c=1/b_len
    best=a.copy(); best_score=internal_score(a)
    for it in range(iters):
        B=A*A
        b=irfft(B, L)[:b_len]
        b = np.clip(b,0,None)
        s=b.sum(); if s <=0: break
        b/=s
        # flatten
        ratio=b/c
        # avoid zero power: ratio = np.maximum(ratio, 1e-300)
        bflat = ratio ** alpha
        bflat /= bflat.sum()
        # maybe randomize slightly: bflat += rng.normal(0, noise, b_len); clip; normalize
        Bf = rfft(bflat, L)
        A2 = np.sqrt(Bf + 0j)
        # align signs with A:
        dot = A2 * np.conj(A)
        mask = np.real(dot) < 0
        A2[mask] *= -1
        # Nyquist real: A2[L//2] = np.sign(real(A2[L//2]*conj(A[l//2])))? If imag small ignore; to keep real, set imag 0? For rfft, Nyquist should be real. Bf Nyq real; sqrt may have imag 0 if real positive, or nan if negative. Bf Nyq = (2 r_a? )? Could be negative. sqrt of negative real gives +i*sqrt(| |), not real, leading a complex? irfft expects Nyq real? It may ignore imag? Better handle: if L even, nyq_re = Bf[Nyq].real; if nyq_re >=0 A2[Nyq]=sqrt(nyq_re), else -sqrt(-nyq_re)? But sign of real root choose align with A. If negative, there are two real roots ±sqrt(abs). Choose align.
        if L%2==0:
           nyq = Bf[L//2].real
           root = np.sqrt(abs(nyq) + 0j)
           if nyq < 0: root = 1j*? Wait A^2 = negative real, A must be purely imaginary, not real, which would make a complex? But for real a, A[Nyq] is real (for rfft). Then A^2[Nyq] is real nonnegative! Because A[Nyq] real, square nonnegative. Therefore Bf Nyq from a conv should be nonnegative. But target Bf from arbitrary bflat may have negative Nyq, no real A exists for that B. To maintain real a, we should enforce Bf Nyq nonnegative: set Bf[Nyq] = abs(Bf[Nyq]) (or 0) before sqrt. This slightly alters bflat sum? Maybe okay.
           sign = 1 if np.real(root * np.conj(A[Nyq])) >=0 else -1
           A2[Nyq] = sign*sqrt(abs(nyq))
        A = A2
        a_new = irfft(A, L)[:n]
        a_new = np.clip(a_new, 0, None)
        s=a_new.sum(); if small: break
        a_new /= s
        # maybe add tiny random noise to break symmetry
        if rng.random()<perturb_prob:
            a_new *= np.exp(rng.normal(0, 0.05, n))
            a_new /= a_new.sum()
        score=internal_score(a_new)
        if score<best_score: best=a_new; best_score=score; A=rfft(best?)? Wait if we accept only best, iteration should continue from current a_new regardless? The projection may oscillate; maybe continue from current and track best. Could add momentum: if score worse than current, revert? Use current = a_new always; because alternating projection path may go temporarily worse but converge. Track best.
        A = rfft(a_new, L)
    return best
```

Let's write helpers:
```
import numpy as np, time, math

def _next_pow2(m):
    p=1
    while p<m: p <<=1
    return p

def _internal_score(a, L):
    b = _conv_real(a, L)
    mb = float(np.max(b[:len?])) # b full L, but after 2n-1 zeros maybe due roundoff; take first 2n-1 and clip.
    if not finite: return inf
    mb = max(mb,0)
    return 2.0*a.size*mb
```

if S<1e-12 return inf

`_correlate_valid(a, q, Lg, idx)`: as described. To avoid allocating qfull each time? Precompute? q changes only. We can allocate `qfull = np.zeros(Lg); qfull[:b_len]=q`. That's O(Lg) per iter; okay. We can compute revq from q via `rq = np.zeros(Lg); rq[:b_len] = q[::-1]?` Wait earlier qfull[:b_len]=q, revq=qfull[::-1] gives revq[:Lb_len]=? For Lg > b_len, qfull = [q..., zeros]; rev = [zeros..., q_{b_len-1},...,q0]. We can construct more efficiently: `revq = np.zeros(Lg); offset = Lg - b_len; revq[offset:offset+b_len] = q[::-1]`. Helper takes `offset`, `idx`.
```
def _correlate_valid(a, q, Lg, offset, idx):
    revq = np.zeros(Lg)
    # copy q reversed into revq[offset:offset+len(q)]
    revq[offset:offset+q.size] = q[::-1]
    C = np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(a, Lg) * np.fft.rfft(revq, Lg), Lg)
    return C[idx]
```
Check idx = Lg-1 - arange(n). For n=2, idx [4,3]; C[4]=a0q0+a1q1 c0, C[3]=a0q1+a1q2 c1. Good.

Need Lg >= n + len(q) -1. offset = Lg - len(q). Since Lg pow2 >= len(q)+n-1, offset >= n-1. Good.

But constructing revq each iteration is O(Lg). OK.

`_project_box(b, T)`:
```
def _project_box(b, T):
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=float).copy()
    s=b.sum(); if s<=0: return uniform
    b /= s
    T = max(T, b_len/(2n-1)? c*1.0000001)
    if np.max(b) <= T: 
        # still maybe improve flatness by moving values toward T? If max below target, we can clip lower? For min max, target not binding; maybe spread mass from higher to lower without exceeding T to reduce variance: find max? Could just return b normalized? But if T larger than max, box not active. To flatten, we can do projection onto [0,T] sum1: minimize distance; if max<=T and some entries very low, projection leaves b unchanged. That's okay; later T lowered until binding.
        return b
    bpm = np.minimum(b, T)
    deficit = 1.0 - bpm.sum()
    if deficit <= 1e-15: return bpm
    mask = bpm < T
    space = T - bpm[mask]
    if space.sum() <= 0: return bpm
    bpm[mask] += deficit * space / space.sum()
    # ensure sum 1
    bpm -= (bpm.sum()-1)/bpm.size? maybe small
    return bpm
```
If deficit > space sum shouldn't happen if T > average? space sum = K*T - sum(bpm) where K count mask; since all non-mask at T. If T>avg, total capacity of all n = (2n-1)T >1, so space sum >= deficit. Good.

`_spectral_project_a_from_btarget(a, btarget, A_current, L, rng, sign_perturb=0.05)`:
```
B = np.fft.rfft(btarget, L)
# fix Nyq nonnegative
if L%2==0:
   nyq = float(np.real(B[L//2]))
   B = B.copy()
   B[L//2] = abs(nyq) + 0j
A2 = np.sqrt(B + 0j)
# align signs
dot = A2 * np.conj(A_current)
mask = np.real(dot) < 0
A2[mask] *= -1
# random perturb signs occasionally
if rng.random() < sign_perturb:
    rand = rng.random(A2.size) < 0.05
    A2[rand] *= -1
# Nyq sign align and real:
if L%2==0:
   root = np.sqrt(abs(nyq))
   if len(A_current)>L//2:
      if np.real(root * np.conj(A_current[L//2].real)) < 0: root=-root
   A2[L//2] = root + 0j
A = A2
a_new = np.fft.irfft(A, L)[:n]
a_new = np.clip(a_new, 0, None)
s = a_new.sum()
if s <= tiny: return a, A
a_new /= s
return a_new, A
```
But if we random flip signs, A may not be consistent with btarget? It is root, so A^2=Btarget still. Good.

We need current A consistent with a? In loop, after a_new, set A = rfft(a_new). But if we use A2 for a_new, its square is Btarget, but after clipping/normalizing, A changes. For sign alignment next, use rfft(a_new).

Spectral box search function:
```
def _spectral_box_search(a_start, n, L, time_limit, rng, floor_score=1.01):
    a = a_start.copy(); a/=sum
    A = rfft(a, L)
    best=a.copy(); best_score=internal_score(a)
    b_len=2n-1; c=1/b_len
    T = max(b)  # current
    T_floor = floor_score/(2*n)
    t0=time.monotonic(); it=0
    while time < t_limit and T > T_floor:
        # Decrease T gradually. Choose new_T = max(T_floor, min(T*0.99, T - small))
        # If many iterations without improvement, slow.
        new_T = max(T_floor, T * 0.99) # maybe too fast at start; use 0.995
        btarget = _project_box(conv(a), new_T)
        # Run inner iterations with this btarget? Could update btarget from current a each inner.
        for inner in range(20):
            bcur = conv(a)
            btarget = _project_box(bcur, new_T)
            a_new, A2 = _spectral_project(a, btarget, A, L, rng)
            a = a_new; A = rfft(a)
            sc=internal_score(a); if sc<best: best...
            if time>limit break
        # after inner, if current max not < old_T*0.98, maybe reject new_T? We still continue.
        old_T=T; T=new_T
        if T == old_T: break
    return best
```
But this always accepts spectral a even if score worse. It might wander. We can track best. The btarget is based on current a, so if a bad, btarget based on bad b but capped at T; spectral may produce better or worse. It should reduce max toward T if feasible.

Algorithm Frank-Wolfe:
```
a current sum1.
while time:
    b=conv(a); active q=softmax((b-mb)/tau)
    g=2*c (or 4n*c)
    # choose candidate vertices: indices with smallest g (or random among bottom K)
    candidates = np.argpartition(g, B)[:B]
    # also random sample some
    best_move=None
    for j in candidates:
        direction = e_j - a
        # line search t
        for t in [0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02]? maybe 1 if can handle:
            a_try = (1-t)*a; a_try[j]+=t
            if score(a_try) < score(a): accept; break
    if no move: perturb a by random small vertex update or logarithmic step; reduce tau; etc.
```

def _lp_polish(a, n, Lb, Lg? no need, time_limit, rng):
    best=a
    K=200/500 active

    K=len(active)
    A = np.empty((K,n), dtype=float)
    bvals = np.empty(K)
    for r,k in enumerate(active):
       bvals[r]=b[k]
       lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
       # j indices lo..hi, coefficient a[k-j]

```

Spectral reach from a start:
```
def _spectral_reach(a, n, L, time_limit, rng, target_score=1.51, max_iter=5000):
   a=normalize(a); A=rfft(a,L)
   b_len=2n-1; c=1/b_len
   best=a; best_score=score
   T = max(b) current
   T_target = max(c*1.01, target_score/(2n)) if target_score < current else current*0.95
   # If current already below target, return.
   while time < limit and T > T_target:
      # reduce T towards target by factor 0.98 maybe
      T = max(T_target, T*0.98)
      b=conv(a); btarget=project_box(b,T)
      # maybe add tiny noise to btarget: btarget += rng.normal(0, 0.01*T, b_len); clip norm
      a_new,A=spectral_project(...)
      a=a_new
      sc=score; update best
   return best
```

`_normalize_nonnegative(x)`:
```
x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
s = x.sum()
if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0: return np.ones(len(x))/len(x)
return x/s
```

`_internal_score(a, L, b_len)`:
```
b = _fft_conv(a,L)
b = b[:b_len]
# clip small negative
if b.size:
    mb = float(np.max(b))
else: return inf
if mb < 0 and mb > -1e-300: mb=0.0
if not finite: return inf
S = float(np.sum(a))
if S < 1e-12: return inf
return float(2.0*a.size*mb/(S*S))
```
Because objective homogeneous. In polish sum=1, but after exact? okay.

`_cache_ctx(n)` maybe in main to avoid recompute. Tuple `(n, Lb, Lg, offset, idx, b_len)`. Helper `_make_ctx(n)`.

def _make_ctx(n):
   b_len=2*n-1
   Lb=_next_pow2(b_len)
   Lg=_next_pow2(b_len+n-1)
   offset=Lg-b_len
   idx=Lg-1-np.arange(n)
   return (n,Lb,Lg,offset,idx,b_len)
```

`_score_ctx(a, ctx)` uses n,Lb,b_len from ctx.

`_try_log_step(a, g, score, ctx, rng, alphas)`:
```
h = g - np.dot(a,g)
maxh = np.max(np.abs(h))
if maxh <= 1e-300: return None
hn = h / maxh
theta = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
for alpha in alphas:
    theta_try = theta - alpha * hn
    # subtract mean for stability
    theta_try -= np.mean(theta_try)
    at = _softmax(theta_try)
    s = _score_ctx(at, ctx)
    if s < score - 1e-12: return at,s
return None
```
Gradient wrt log of score factor 4n*c; g = c, missing constant but direction same. h=a? For softmax, gradient is a*(grad_a - dot(a,grad_a)). If grad_a ∝ g, h = a*(g - dot(a,g))? Wait earlier formula: dL/dtheta_j = a_j (g_j - Σ a_l g_l). I omitted factor a_j. If g is gradient wrt a, then yes multiply by a_j. In our `c` is `∂b/∂a` factor maybe, gradient wrt a ∝ g = c. So h should be `a * (g - np.dot(a,g))`. I wrote h = g - dot; missing a factor. Without a, update in theta not mirror. Let's derive carefully.
Let a(theta)=softmax(theta). da_l/dtheta_j = a_l(δ_{jl}-a_j). Gradient wrt theta_j = Σ_l grad_a_l a_l(δ_{lj}-a_j)= grad_a_j a_j - a_j Σ_l grad_a_l a_l = a_j (g_j - a·g). So h = a*(g - dot). Good. If we use h = g - dot (not multiplied), that's gradient in additive projection not log. For softmax update, multiply by a. Since a entries ~1/n, h ~1/n^2, step needs larger scaling. We normalize h by max, so no matter. Use `h = a * (g - np.dot(a,g))`.
Then `theta - alpha*h_norm` with h_norm max1. Good.
Alternatively additive gradient step direction in a: d = g - mean(g) (tangent); update a_try = clip(a - eta*d)/sum. Could also try. But log step okay.

Need check sign: We want decrease score. If g_j high (entry contributes to peaks), a·g dot; h_j = a_j(g_j - mean_a g). For high g, h positive; theta decreases -> a decreases. Good.

Could also try additive step: `d = g - np.dot(a,g)`? Actually tangent with Euclidean L2 projection: d = g - mean(g) (unweighted). But mean maybe; update a_try=clip(a - eta*d)/sum. We can include if log fails. But maybe not needed.

`_spectral_project(a, btarget, A_current, L, rng, perturb=0.02)`:
Input a, btarget (sum1). Returns a_new, A. Use rfft. Need ensure `A_current` maybe not consistent? It is rfft(a).
```
B = np.fft.rfft(btarget, L)
if L %2 ==0:
   nyq_re = float(np.real(B[L//2]))
   B = B.copy()
   B[L//2] = abs(nyq_re) + 0j
A2 = np.sqrt(B + 0j)
# align signs
if A_current is not None and A_current.size == A2.size:
   dot = A2 * np.conj(A_current)
   mask = np.real(dot) < 0
   A2[mask] *= -1
# random sign perturb
if rng.random() < perturb:
   flip = rng.random(A2.size) < 0.01
   A2[flip] *= -1
if L%2==0:
   root = np.sqrt(abs(nyq_re))
   if A_current is not None:
       prev = float(np.real(A_current[L//2]))
       if root * prev < 0: root = -root
   A2[L//2] = root + 0j
a_new = np.fft.irfft(A2, L)[:a.size]
a_new = np.clip(a_new, 0, None)
s = a_new.sum()
if s <= 1e-300:
   return a, A_current
a_new /= s
A_new = np.fft.rfft(a_new, L)
return a_new, A_new
```
But if we set A2 signs then compute A_new from a_new, the square of A_new not Btarget; for next alignment use A_new. Good.

Potential issue: random sign flip of many frequencies can make a_new mostly negative with tiny positive sum; then bad. Use small perturb 0.005 and only if current score not improving? Maybe in spectral reach, use sign perturb 0.01. If sum too low, fallback.

`_project_box(b, T)`: as above. Need ensure b sum 1 before? It may be from FFT sum slightly not 1; normalize.
```
def _project_box(b, T):
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=float)
    b = np.maximum(b,0)
    s=b.sum()
    if s<=0: return np.full_like(b,1/b.size)
    b = b/s
    if T < b.mean(): T=b.mean()
    if T <= b.max():
       bcap = np.minimum(b,T)
       deficit = 1 - bcap.sum()
       if deficit <= 1e-12: return bcap
       mask = bcap < T
       if mask.sum()==0: return bcap
       space = T - bcap[mask]
       cap_space = space.sum()
       if cap_space <= 0: return bcap
       bcap[mask] += deficit * space / cap_space
       # normalize to 1
       bcap /= bcap.sum()
       return bcap
    else:
       # If max below T, we can still project toward uniform without increasing max above T:
       # Water-filling upward? To reduce variance, we can move mass from entries > average to entries < average but keep max<=T.
       # Simpler: blend with uniform: 0.5 b +0.5 uniform. But spectral reach calls with T<=current max until floor, so maybe max binding. At final T floor less than max? We can just uniform blend.
       return 0.5*b + 0.5*(1.0/b.size)
```
If max below T but T low, we can force uniform: `return np.full_like(b, 1/b.size)`. Better as T approaches floor.

In spectral reach, T decreases from current max to target. At first T=current max; project returns b (maybe no cap). Next T lower than max, cap binds. Good.

`_spectral_reach(a, ctx, time_limit, rng, target_score)`:
```
def _spectral_reach(a, ctx, time_limit, rng, target_score=1.505):
   n,Lb,Lg,offset,idx,b_len=ctx
   a=_normalize_nonnegative(a)
   A=np.fft.rfft(a,Lb)
   best=a.copy(); best_score=_internal_score(a,ctx)
   b=_fft_conv(a,Lb)[:b_len]; mb=float(np.max(np.maximum(b,0)))
   T = mb
   T_floor = max(b_len? c*(1+1e-6), target_score/(2*n)) # c=1/b_len
   # If target_score >= best_score, maybe just one init
   t0=time.monotonic()
   # choose reduction factor; maybe 0.985
   while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit and T > T_floor:
       T = max(T_floor, T * 0.985)
       for inner in range(10):
           b = _fft_conv(a, Lb)[:b_len]
           b = np.maximum(b,0)
           s=b.sum(); if s>0: b/=s
           btarget = _project_box(b, T)
           # add tiny noise for symmetry break
           if rng.random()<0.1:
              noise = rng.normal(0, 0.02*T, b_len)
              btarget = btarget + noise
              btarget = np.maximum(btarget,0)
              st=btarget.sum(); if st>0: btarget/=st
           a_new, A = _spectral_project(a,btarget,A,Lb,rng,perturb=0.01)
           a = a_new
           sc = _internal_score(a,ctx)
           if sc < best_score:
              best=a.copy(); best_score=sc
           if time.monotonic()-t0 >= time_limit: break
       # If current score didn't improve after T reduction? continue
   return best, best_score
```
But calling `_fft_conv` and spectral another rfft each inner: 2 FFTs. 5000 iterations maybe okay. We can limit time.

Maybe target_score should be a bit above final target to allow lower bound; use 1.505? We want final <1.5027; T_floor target_score/(2n). If target_score=1.48, T lower, may overconstrain. Use 1.505 for spectral, then polish can get below. But if target upper bound 1.5027, final must <1.5027. We can set spectral target 1.50 to push.

However, exact lower bound ~1; T floor for 1.50 is 0.75/n. Average c=1/(2n-1)≈0.5/n. Feasible. Good.

LP implementation details with SciPy:
```
def _lp_polish(a, ctx, time_limit, rng, K=250, max_iter=200):
    n,Lb,... = ctx
    a = _normalize(a)
    best=a; best_score=_internal_score(a,ctx)
    t0=time.monotonic()
    it=0
    while time < limit and it < max_iter:
        b = _fft_conv(a,Lb)[:b_len]
        b=np.maximum(b,0); b/=b.sum()
        M=b.max()
        if M <= 0: break
        # choose active top 2K
        top_size=min(2*K, b_len)
        if top_size < b_len:
            top = np.argpartition(b, top_size-1)[-top_size:]
            active = rng.choice(top, K, replace=False)
        else: active=np.arange(b_len)
        active = np.sort(active) maybe.
        # build A
        Arows = np.empty((K,n))
        bvals = np.empty(K)
        for r,k in enumerate(active):
            bvals[r]=b[k]
            lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
            Arows[r, lo:hi+1] = a[lo? wait coefficient a[k-j]
            # j range lo..hi. For j=lo, k-lo; j=hi,k-hi. Ascending j coefficient descending indices.
            Arows[r, lo:hi+1] = a[k-hi:k-lo+1][::-1]
        # LP
        c=np.zeros(n+1); c[-1]=1.0
        A_ub=np.empty((K,n+1)); A_ub[:,:n]=2.0*Arows; A_ub[:,-1]=-1.0
        A_eq=np.ones((1,n+1)); A_eq[0,-1]=0
        ymax = min(0.1, max(0.01, 3.0/np.sqrt(n)))
        bounds=[(0.0,ymax)]*n + [(0.0, None)]
        res = _linprog(c, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=bvals, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=[1.0], bounds=bounds, method='highs')
        if res.success:
           y=res.x[:n]
           y=_normalize_nonnegative(y)
           # line search
           accepted=False
           for lam in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625, 0.03125):
              atr = (1-lam)*a + lam*y
              atr=_normalize_nonnegative(atr)
              sc=_internal_score(atr,ctx)
              if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                  a=atr; best=atr.copy(); best_score=sc; accepted=True; break
        # if no accept and it%10==0: perturb a
        if not accepted:
           if it % 5 == 0:
              ap=_random_perturb(a, 0.05, rng)
              if _internal_score(ap,ctx) < best_score:
                  a=ap; best=ap.copy(); best_score=_internal_score(ap)
        it+=1
    return best,best_score
```
Potential issue: LP with K and y_max may return y extreme (sparse) but line search to y may increase; if no accept, no progress. Could try using LP solution as direction but with small lambda; included.

LP active constraints all with bvals near M; objective min τ. Since feasible y=a gives τ=M. It might find y with τ slightly lower. Good.

Build Arows dense O(K*n). For K=300,n=8192 ~2.5M; per LP 20MB. If 100 LPs, okay. But SciPy linprog may be slow; use K=100 for n>4096. Adaptive: `K = 120 if n>5000 else 250`. Time limit. Also if linprog not available skip.

Need ensure `linprog` doesn't take too long per call; maybe set `options={'time_limit': 5}`? Highs options time? `linprog(..., options={'presolve': True})`. Could set `options={'time_limit': 2}`? HiGHS supports time_limit? Maybe. To avoid hanging, not critical.

Now, let's examine FW gradient sign and step. We compute q=softmax((b-mb)/tau). If tau too small, q at one peak. Then c_j =Σ_l a_l q_{j+l}. For a peak at lag k*, c_j is like reverse peak shifted; gradient high for entries that pair with peak. Adding mass to low c entries reduces active peak? def _optimize_start(a, ctx, budget, rng, do_lp=True):
    a = _normalize_nonnegative(a)
    if a.size ==0: return a
    t0=time.monotonic(); limit=t0+max(0,budget)
    sc = _score_ctx(a, ctx)
    # spectral phase if sc > 1.62 and budget > 20
    target_sp = 1.505
    if sc > target_sp + 0.05 and budget > 30:
        spec_budget = min(60.0, budget*0.25)
        a, sc = _spectral_reach(a, ctx, spec_budget, rng, target_score=target_sp)
    # fw phase
    fw_budget = max(0, limit - time.monotonic())
    if do_lp:
       fw_budget = max(0, fw_budget - min(30.0, budget*0.15))
    a = _fw_optimize(a, ctx, fw_budget, rng)
    sc=_score_ctx(a,ctx)
    # lp polish if sc < 1.65 and linprog and time left
    if _HAS_LINPROG and sc < 1.65:
       lp_budget = max(0, limit - time.monotonic())
       if lp_budget > 20:
           a_lp = _lp_polish(a, ctx, min(lp_budget, 60.0), rng)
           sc_lp = _score_ctx(a_lp, ctx)
           if sc_lp < sc: a=a_lp; sc=sc_lp
    return a
```

def _fw_optimize(a, ctx, time_limit, rng):
   n=ctx[0]; ...
   a=_normalize_nonnegative(a)
   best=a.copy(); best_score=_score_ctx(a,ctx)
   last_exact=time.monotonic()
   sharpness=30.0
   no_improve=0
   t0=time.monotonic(); limit=t0+max(0,time_limit)
   it=0
   t_values_vk = (0.08,0.03,0.01,0.003)
   while time.monotonic() < limit:
       b = _fft_conv(a, Lb)[:b_len]
       b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
       mb = float(np.max(b)) if b.size else 0.0
       score = float(2*n*mb/(a.sum()**2))? a sum1; use _score_ctx? But we already have b; to avoid recompute, compute score directly.
       S=a.sum(); if S<... score=inf else score=2*n*mb/(S*S)
       if score < best_score - 1e-12: best=a.copy(); best_score=score
       if mb <= 0: break
       tau = mb / sharpness
       q = _make_q(b, mb, sharpness)
       c = _correlate_valid(a, q, ctx)
       g = c  # up to factor
       # candidate list

       accepted = False

       if not accepted:

          if n > 10:
             low = np.argpartition(g, 5)[:5]
             high = np.argpartition(-g, 5)[:5]
             pairs = [(int(high[x]), int(low[y])) for x in range(min(3,len(high))) for y in range(min(3,len(low)))]
             for i,j in pairs:

                   if res: a,sc=res; accepted=True; break

          it +=1
          # gradually sharpen

       else:
          no_improve +=1
          if no_improve == 20:

          elif no_improve % 10 == 0:

                a=ap
       # exact update? Could evaluate current if it % 100 or 1s and internal best maybe.
       # To keep code simple, no exact here.
   return best

def _search_n(n, deadline, rng, base_arr, best_seq, best_score):
   t0=time.monotonic(); total_budget = max(0, deadline - t0)
   # We'll run runs until deadline - 5s.
   runs = []
   # start 0: uniform spectral
   runs.append(('uniform', np.ones(n)/n))
   if base_arr is not None and base_arr.size == n:
       runs.append(('base', _normalize_nonnegative(base_arr)))
   runs.append(('dirichlet', _dirichlet_start(n,1.0,rng)))
   runs.append(('exp', _exp_center_start(n,rng)))
   if best_seq not None and len(best_seq)==n:
       runs.append(('best', np.array(best_seq)/sum))
   # Allocate time: first run 40%, remaining 60/len? Or equal.
   # For each run, budget = min( (deadline - now) / (remaining runs +1) maybe plus first bigger)
   for idx,a in enumerate(runs):
       rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
       if rem < 20: break
       remaining_runs = len(runs)-idx
       # give at least 30 if possible; allocate rem / remaining_runs; first can be 1.5x
       budget = rem / remaining_runs
       if idx==0: budget = min(rem*0.45, max(budget, 60))
       if budget < 10: budget = rem
       a_opt = _optimize_start(a, ctx, budget, rng, do_lp=(n<=8192 and _HAS_LINPROG))
       best_seq,best_score = _update_best(np.asarray(a_opt), ctx, best_seq, best_score)
       # optionally feed a_opt as next perturbed start if time left
   return best_seq,best_score
```

Let's write code now mentally, ensuring no syntax errors.

Detailed code:
```
import numpy as np
import time
try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog as _linprog
    _HAS_LINPROG = True
except Exception:
    _linprog = None
    _HAS_LINPROG = False

def _next_pow2(m):
    p=1
    while p < m:
        p <<= 1
    return p
```

`_make_ctx` returns tuple. Use Python tuple; unpack.

`_normalize_nonnegative(x)`:
```
def _normalize_nonnegative(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.ndim != 1:
       x = x.ravel()
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
    s = float(x.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
       if x.size == 0:
          return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
       return np.full(x.size, 1.0/x.size, dtype=np.float64)
    x = x / s
    return x
```

`_fft_conv(a,L)`:
```
def _fft_conv(a,L):
    A=np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    return np.fft.irfft(A*A, L)
```

`_score_ctx(a,ctx)`:
```
def _score_ctx(a,ctx):
    n,Lb,Lg,offset,idx,b_len = ctx
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-300: return float('inf')
    b = _fft_conv(a, Lb)
    b = b[:b_len]
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    if b.size ==0: return float('inf')
    mb=float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(mb): return float('inf')
    return float(2.0*n*mb/(S*S))
```
This recomputes conv; okay.

`_softmax`:
```
def _softmax(theta):
    theta=np.asarray(theta,dtype=np.float64)
    z=theta - float(np.max(theta))
    a=np.exp(z)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(theta.size, 1.0/theta.size)
    return a/s
```

`_correlate_valid`:
```
def _correlate_valid(a,q,ctx):
    n,Lb,Lg,offset,idx,b_len=ctx
    revq=np.zeros(Lg, dtype=np.float64)
    # q length b_len
    revq[offset:offset+q.size] = q[::-1]
    C=np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(a, Lg) * np.fft.rfft(revq, Lg), Lg)
    return C[idx]
```
Ensure `q.size == b_len`; if not, `revq[offset:offset+q.size]`.

`_make_q`:
```
def _make_q(b, mb, sharpness):
    if mb <= 0 or sharpness <= 0:
        return np.full(b.size, 1.0/b.size)
    tau = mb / float(sharpness)
    x = (b - mb) / tau
    x = x - float(np.max(x))
    q = np.exp(x)
    s = float(q.sum())
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(b.size, 1.0/b.size)
    return q/s
```

`_project_box`:
```
def _project_box(b, T):
    b=np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
    s=float(b.sum())
    m=b.size
    if s <= 1e-300 or m <=0:
       return np.full(m,1.0/m)
    b = b/s
    avg = 1.0/m
    if T < avg: T=avg
    if float(np.max(b)) <= T:
       # force flatness by mixing to uniform as T approaches avg
       blend = min(1.0, (T - b.min()) / (avg - b.min() + 1e-300))? maybe too complex.
       # If T close to avg, uniform; else return b
       ratio = T / avg
       if ratio < 10.0:
           # linearly mix: weight = (10 - ratio)/(9)?? At ratio=1, uniform; at >=10, no mix.
           alpha = max(0.0, min(1.0, (10.0 - ratio)/9.0))
           b = (1-alpha)*b + alpha*avg
           b = np.maximum(b,0); b/=b.sum()
       return b
    bcap = np.minimum(b, T)
    deficit = 1.0 - float(bcap.sum())
    if deficit <= 1e-12:
        return bcap
    mask = bcap < T
    if not np.any(mask):
        return bcap
    space = T - bcap[mask]
    cap_space = float(space.sum())
    if cap_space <= 1e-300:
        return bcap
    if deficit > cap_space:
       # set all to T? not possible; but T>avg so not
       bcap = np.full(m, T)
       bcap /= bcap.sum()
       return bcap
    bcap[mask] += deficit * space / cap_space
    bcap = np.maximum(bcap, 0.0)
    bcap /= bcap.sum()
    return bcap
```
The mixing when max<=T arbitrary; okay.

`_spectral_project`:
```
def _spectral_project(a,btarget,A,L,rng,perturb=0.01):
    B=np.fft.rfft(btarget, L)
    nyq_abs = 0.0
    if L % 2 == 0:
        nyq_abs = abs(float(np.real(B[L//2])))
        B = B.copy()
        B[L//2] = nyq_abs + 0j
    A2 = np.sqrt(B + 0j)
    if A is not None and A.size == A2.size:
        dot = A2 * np.conj(A)
        mask = np.real(dot) < 0.0
        if np.any(mask): A2[mask] *= -1.0
    if rng is not None and rng.random() < perturb:
        flip = rng.random(A2.size) < 0.01
        if np.any(flip): A2[flip] *= -1.0
    if L % 2 == 0:
        root = float(np.sqrt(nyq_abs))
        if A is not None and A.size > L//2:
            prev = float(np.real(A[L//2]))
            if root * prev < 0: root = -root
        A2[L//2] = root + 0j
    a_new = np.fft.irfft(A2, L)[:a.size]
    a_new = np.clip(a_new, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s=float(a_new.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return a, A
    a_new = a_new / s
    A_new = np.fft.rfft(a_new, L)
    return a_new, A_new
```
Potential: `A2 = np.sqrt(B + 0j)` returns complex; for B with negative real non-Nyq, A2 has imaginary; fine (real time inverse if half arbitrary). For Nyq, B Nyq set nonnegative real, root real.

`_spectral_reach`:
```
def _spectral_reach(a, ctx, time_limit, rng, target_score=1.505):
    n,Lb,Lg,offset,idx,b_len=ctx
    a=_normalize_nonnegative(a)
    if a.size==0 or time_limit <=0: return a, _score_ctx(a,ctx)
    t_end = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(time_limit))
    A=np.fft.rfft(a, Lb)
    best=a.copy()
    best_score=_score_ctx(a,ctx)
    T = max(_score_ctx(a,ctx)/(2.0*n), 1e-15) # current max since sum1
    avg = 1.0/b_len
    T_floor = max(avg * (1.0 + 1e-6), target_score/(2.0*n))
    if T <= T_floor:
       return best,best_score
    outer=0
    while time.monotonic() < t_end and T > T_floor and outer < 10000:
       T = max(T_floor, T * 0.985)
       inner_iters = 8
       for _ in range(inner_iters):
          if time.monotonic() >= t_end: break
          b = _fft_conv(a, Lb)[:b_len]
          b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
          s=float(b.sum())
          if s > 1e-300: b = b/s
          else: b = np.full(b_len, 1.0/b_len)
          btarget = _project_box(b, T)
          if rng is not None and rng.random() < 0.15:
             noise = rng.normal(0.0, max(1e-15, 0.05*T), b_len)
             btarget = btarget + noise
             btarget = np.maximum(btarget, 0.0)
             nb=float(btarget.sum())
             if nb > 1e-300: btarget = btarget/nb
          a, A = _spectral_project(a, btarget, A, Lb, rng, perturb=0.01)
          sc=_score_ctx(a,ctx)
          if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
             best=a.copy(); best_score=sc
       T_cur = max(_score_ctx(a,ctx)/(2*n), avg) # maybe expensive; use best_score? We can track T from best? Use current score.
       # To avoid extra score, we already computed sc current? last sc available but if inner loop not run? Use variable.
       # Simpler: if best_score changed? We can set T = min(T, max(best_score/(2n), T_floor))
       if best_score < T*(2*n):
           T = max(T_floor, best_score/(2*n))
       outer += 1
    return best,best_score
```
We compute `_score_ctx` multiple. In inner loop, after spectral, we compute sc. Keep `current_score=sc`. Use it to update T: `if current_score < 2*n*T: T = max(T_floor, current_score/(2*n))`.
Need initialize `current_score=best_score`.

But `T = max(best_score/(2n))` may jump down if best is lower. Good.

`_spectral_project` uses `a.size` as n; if a normalized. Good.

`_dirichlet_start`:
```
def _dirichlet_start(n, alpha, rng):
   if n <=0: return np.ones(1)
   x = rng.gamma(alpha, 1.0, size=n)
   s=x.sum()
   if s>0: return x/s
   return np.full(n,1.0/n)
```
`rng.gamma` with small alpha okay (alpha >0). For alpha=0.1, gamma produces many small, some large; may be very sparse. Good.

`_exp_center_start`:
```
def _exp_center_start(n, rng):
   p = int(rng.integers(0,n))
   beta = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 5.0))
   x = np.exp(-beta * np.abs(np.arange(n) - p))
   s=x.sum()
   if s>0: return x/s
   return np.full(n,1.0/n)
```

`_geometric_start`:
```
def _geometric_start(n, rng):
   r = float(rng.choice([0.4,0.6,0.8,1.2,1.5,2.0]))
   x = np.power(r, np.arange(n, dtype=float))
   s=x.sum()
   if s>0 and finite: return x/s
   # fallback reverse
   x = np.power(0.5, np.arange(n, dtype=float)); return x/sum
```
Maybe include but starts enough.

`_fw_candidates`:
```
def _fw_candidates(g, rng, n, bcand=8, rcand=4):
    if n ==0: return []
    if n <= bcand + rcand:
       return np.arange(n).tolist()
    bc = int(min(bcand, n-1))
    low = np.argpartition(g, bc-1)[:bc]
    rand = rng.integers(0,n,size=rcand)
    # include boundaries
    extra = np.array([0, n-1])
    cands = np.unique(np.concatenate((low, rand, extra)))
    return cands.tolist()
```
If g contains NaN, argpartition may issues; g finite. Add fallback if not finite: `if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)): return [int(rng.integers(0,n))]`.

`_try_vertex`:
```
def _try_vertex(a,j,score,ctx,t_values):
    for t in t_values:
       at = a * (1.0 - t)
       at[j] += t
       # ensure finite/nonneg
       if at.sum() <=0: continue
       at = at / at.sum() # should be 1
       sc = _score_ctx(at, ctx)
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < score - 1e-12:
          return at, sc
    return None
```
Scaling by 1-t preserves sum t? Sum at = (1-t)*1 + t =1 exactly; normalization small. Good.

`_try_pair`:
```
def _try_pair(a,i,j,score,ctx,fracs):
    for f in fracs:
       delta = f * a[i]
       if delta <=0: continue
       if delta > a[i]: delta=a[i]
       at = a.copy()
       at[i] -= delta
       at[j] += delta
       if at[i] < 0: at[i]=0; at[j]+= -? Actually subtract exact.
       s=at.sum()
       if s<=0: continue
       at /= s
       sc=_score_ctx(at,ctx)
       if sc < score - 1e-12: return at,sc
    return None
```
Since delta<=a[i], nonnegative.

`_try_log_step`:
```
def _try_log_step(a,g,score,ctx,alphas):
    if a.size ==0: return None
    mean = float(np.dot(a,g))
    h = a * (g - mean)
    maxh = float(np.max(np.abs(h)))
    if maxh <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(maxh): return None
    hn = h / maxh
    theta = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    for alpha in alphas:
       theta_try = theta - alpha * hn
       m = float(np.mean(theta_try))
       theta_try -= m
       at = _softmax(theta_try)
       sc = _score_ctx(at, ctx)
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < score - 1e-12:
          return at, sc
    return None
```
Factor missing 2n etc irrelevant.

Now `_lp_polish` code. It needs `_linprog` available. It can call `_score_ctx` for line search. Use best internal.
```
def _lp_polish(a, ctx, time_limit, rng):
    if not _HAS_LINPROG: return a
    n,...=ctx
    a=_normalize_nonnegative(a)
    best=a.copy(); best_score=_score_ctx(a,ctx)
    t_end=time.monotonic()+max(0,time_limit)
    K = 120 if n>5000 else 240
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() < t_end and it < 500:
       b=_fft_conv(a,Lb)[:b_len]; b=np.maximum(b,0); s=b.sum(); if s>0: b/=s
       M=float(np.max(b))
       if not finite or M<=0: break
       top_size=min(2*K,b_len)
       if top_size < b_len:
          top=np.argpartition(b, top_size-1)[-top_size:]
          active=rng.choice(top, K, replace=False)
       else: active=np.arange(b_len)
       active=np.asarray(active)
       Arows=np.empty((K,n), dtype=np.float64)
       bvals=np.empty(K, dtype=np.float64)
       for r,k in enumerate(active):
          k=int(k); bvals[r]=b[k]
          lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
          row = a[k-hi:k-lo+1][::-1]
          Arows[r, lo:hi+1] = row
       c=np.zeros(n+1); c[-1]=1.0
       A_ub=np.empty((K,n+1)); A_ub[:,:n]=2.0*Arows; A_ub[:,-1]=-1.0
       A_eq=np.ones((1,n+1)); A_eq[0,-1]=0.0
       b_eq=np.ones(1)
       ymax = min(0.25, max(0.03, 2.5*float(np.max(a))))
       bounds = [(0.0,ymax)]*n + [(0.0,None)]
       try:
          res=_linprog(c, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=bvals, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=b_eq, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve': True})
       except Exception:
          res=None
       accepted=False
       if res is not None and getattr(res,'success',False):
          y=np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=np.float64)
          y=_normalize_nonnegative(y)
          for lam in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625,0.03125):
             at=(1.0-lam)*a + lam*y
             at=_normalize_nonnegative(at)
             sc=_score_ctx(at,ctx)
             if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                a=at; best=at.copy(); best_score=sc; accepted=True; break
       if not accepted:
          if it % 5 == 0:
             ap=_random_perturb(a, 0.05, rng)
             sc=_score_ctx(ap,ctx)
             if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                a=ap; best=ap.copy(); best_score=sc
          elif it % 10 == 0:
             # rerun with smaller K? or break if many
             pass
       it+=1
       if it % 20 ==0:
          # if no improvement long? We can track but simple
          pass
    return best
```
Memory: `Arows` K*n. If n=16384,K=120 ->1.9M, 15MB. okay. But `bounds` list length n+1 of tuples large but okay. For n=16k, 16k tuples memory ~1MB.

`_exact_update`:
```
def _exact_update(a, ctx, best_seq, best_score):
    if a is None: return best_seq,best_score
    a = _normalize_nonnegative(a)
    if a.size == 0: return best_seq,best_score
    seq = [float(x) for x in a]
    try:
       sc = float(evaluate_sequence(seq))
    except Exception:
       sc = float('inf')
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
       return seq, sc
    return best_seq,best_score
```
But if best_seq is list, length maybe different; return new list when improved.

`_optimize_start` uses `_spectral_reach` target 1.505. If target too aggressive, maybe. It can pass `target_score=min(1.505, sc*0.95?)`? If current sc=2, target1.505. If current sc=1.6, target1.505. Good. If sc already 1.49, no spectral.
```
def _optimize_start(a, ctx, budget, rng, do_lp=True):
    a=_normalize_nonnegative(a)
    if a.size==0: return a
    total_budget=float(budget)
    t_end=time.monotonic()+total_budget
    sc=_score_ctx(a,ctx)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
       a=np.full(a.size,1.0/a.size); sc=_score_ctx(a,ctx)
    if sc > 1.55 and total_budget > 20:
       spec_budget = min(90.0, total_budget*0.3)
       if spec_budget > 10:
          a, sc = _spectral_reach(a, ctx, spec_budget, rng, target_score=1.505)
    fw_budget = max(0.0, t_end - time.monotonic())
    if do_lp and _HAS_LINPROG and a.size <= 16384:
       reserved = min(60.0, fw_budget*0.2)
       fw_budget = max(0.0, fw_budget - reserved)
    a = _fw_optimize(a, ctx, fw_budget, rng)
    sc=_score_ctx(a,ctx)
    if do_lp and _HAS_LINPROG and sc < 1.70:
       rem = max(0.0, t_end - time.monotonic())
       if rem > 15:
          a_lp = _lp_polish(a, ctx, min(rem, 60.0), rng)
          sc_lp = _score_ctx(a_lp, ctx)
          if np.isfinite(sc_lp) and sc_lp < sc:
             a = a_lp
    return a
```
If `do_lp` true but no time for LP due FW using all; reserved only if LP available. But if FW time 0? okay.

`_search_n`:
```
def _search_n(n, budget, rng, base_arr, best_seq, best_score, target=1.5027):
    t_end=time.monotonic()+max(0,budget)
    ctx=_make_ctx(n)
    starts=[]
    starts.append(('uniform', np.full(n,1.0/n)))
    if base_arr is not None and base_arr.size == n:
       starts.append(('base', _normalize_nonnegative(base_arr)))
    starts.append(('dirichlet', _dirichlet_start(n, 1.0, rng)))
    starts.append(('exp', _exp_center_start(n, rng)))
    # maybe second dirichlet sparse
    starts.append(('sparse', _dirichlet_start(n, 0.3, rng)))
    # remove too many if budget small: max_runs = 1 if budget<120 else 2 if<300 else 4
    max_runs = 1 if t_end - time.monotonic() < 150 else (2 if <400 else len(starts))
    starts = starts[:max_runs]
    for idx, (name,a0) in enumerate(starts):
       rem = t_end - time.monotonic()
       if rem < 10: break
       remaining = len(starts)-idx
       # allocate dyn: first run 45% of rem if remaining>1 else rem; others rem/remaining maybe
       if remaining ==1:
          budget_run=rem
       else:
          if idx==0:
             budget_run = min(rem*0.45, max(rem/remaining, 60.0))
          else:
             budget_run = rem / remaining
       # ensure not too small
       if budget_run < 5: budget_run=rem
       a_opt = _optimize_start(a0, ctx, budget_run, rng, do_lp=(n<=8192 and _HAS_LINPROG))
       best_seq,best_score = _exact_update(a_opt, ctx, best_seq, best_score)
       # if below target, maybe stop further starts for this n? Could continue to improve.
    return best_seq,best_score
```
If base_arr size not n, not included. If best_seq from previous n length n? For n=4096 first none. For optional rerun same n, we could include best_seq. In propose dynamic, after first n maybe if continue same n, we could pass best seq and include perturbed. Not necessary.

Potential: `_search_n` with max_runs 5 each calling spectral/FW might be too much. But time slices handle.

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    total_deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0, float(budget_s) - 5.0)
    best_seq=[]; best_score=float('inf')
    base_raw = globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
    base_arr=None
    if base_raw is not None:
       try:
          base_arr=np.asarray(base_raw,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
          base_arr=np.where(np.isfinite(base_arr),base_arr,0)
          s=base_arr.sum()
          if s>0: base_arr/=s
          else: base_arr=None
       except Exception: base_arr=None
    # Try primary n
    target=1.5027
    # choose n1
    n1=4096
    if base_arr is not None and 3000 <= base_arr.size <= 5000:
       n1=base_arr.size
    # allocate times based on total budget
    t0=time.monotonic()
    total_budget=max(0,total_deadline-t0)
    # Plan: n1 55%, 8192 30%, if still needed 16384 or repeat 15%
    alloc1=min(total_budget*0.55, 900.0)
    if total_budget < 300: alloc1=total_budget
    best_seq,best_score=_search_n(n1, alloc1, rng, base_arr, best_seq,best_score)
    if time.monotonic() < total_deadline and best_score > target:
       n2=8192 if n1 !=8192 else 4096
       alloc2=min(total_deadline-time.monotonic(), total_budget*0.30)
       if alloc2>60:
          best_seq,best_score=_search_n(n2, alloc2, rng, base_arr, best_seq,best_score)
    if time.monotonic() < total_deadline and best_score > target:
       rem=total_deadline-time.monotonic()
       if rem>120:
          # try larger or rerun primary
          n3=16384 if n1<16384 else 8192
          if n3 == n1 or n3 == 8192:
             # maybe rerun n1 with best? but _search_n doesn't use best_seq as start. Could add best if length matches.
          best_seq,best_score=_search_n(n3, min(rem, total_budget*0.20), rng, base_arr, best_seq,best_score)
    # fallback if no valid
    if not best_seq:
       # constant sequence length n1
       n_f = n1 if n1>0 else 4096
       best_seq = [1.0/n_f] * n_f # or [1.0]*n_f
       try: _=evaluate_sequence(best_seq)
       except Exception: pass
    return best_seq
```

`_exact_update(a, best_seq, best_score)`:
```
def _exact_update(a, best_seq, best_score):
    if a is None:
       return best_seq,best_score
    try:
       arr=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
       return best_seq,best_score
    if arr.size==0: return best_seq,best_score
    arr=_normalize_nonnegative(arr)
    seq=[float(x) for x in arr]
    try:
       sc=float(evaluate_sequence(seq))
    except Exception:
       sc=float('inf')
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
       return seq, sc
    return best_seq,best_score
```

```
def _resample_sequence(seq, n, rng):
   x=np.asarray(seq,float); x=_normalize_nonnegative(x)
   if x.size==0: return np.full(n,1.0/n)
   if x.size==n: return x
   xi=np.linspace(0, x.size-1, n)
   xo=np.linspace(0, x.size-1, x.size)
   y=np.interp(xi, xo, x)
   # add small noise
   y=np.maximum(y,0); s=y.sum(); if s>0: y/=s
   return y
```
Then in `_search_n`, if best_seq not None, include resampled best (perturbed) as start for any n. That improves continuity across lengths. Add helper `_resample_sequence`.

Helper `_resample_sequence` top-level:
```
def _resample_sequence(seq, n):
    arr=np.asarray(seq,float).ravel()
    if arr.size==0: return np.full(n,1.0/n)
    arr=_normalize_nonnegative(arr)
    if arr.size == n: return arr
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0, arr.size-1.0, arr.size)
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0, arr.size-1.0, n)
    y=np.interp(x_new, x_old, arr)
    y=np.maximum(y,0.0); s=float(y.sum()); if s>0: y/=s
    return y
```

def _fw_optimize(a, ctx, time_limit, rng):
    n,Lb,Lg,offset,idx,b_len=ctx
    a=_normalize_nonnegative(a)
    best=a.copy()
    best_score=_score_ctx(a,ctx)
    exact_seq=[]; exact_score=float('inf')
    last_exact=time.monotonic()
    if time_limit <=0:
       exact_seq, exact_score = _exact_update(a, exact_seq, exact_score) if n<=8192 else ...
       return best, exact_seq, exact_score
    t_end=...
    sharpness=40.0
    no_improve=0
    it=0
    exact_interval = 2.0 if n<=4096 else (5.0 if n<=8192 else float('inf'))
    while time.monotonic() < t_end:
       b=_fft_conv(a,Lb)[:b_len]
       b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
       S=float(a.sum())
       if S <= 1e-300: b_sum? reset
       mb=float(np.max(b))
       score = float(2.0*n*mb/(S*S))
       if not np.isfinite(score): a=best.copy(); continue
       if score < best_score - 1e-12:
           best=a.copy(); best_score=score
           if exact_interval != float('inf') and (time.monotonic()-last_exact > exact_interval or score < best_score + 1e-9): # just updated best
               exact_seq, exact_score = _exact_update(a, exact_seq, exact_score)
               last_exact=time.monotonic()
       elif exact_interval != float('inf') and time.monotonic()-last_exact > exact_interval:
           exact_seq, exact_score = _exact_update(best, exact_seq, exact_score)
           last_exact=time.monotonic()
       # q, grad...
       # attempts...
       if accepted: ...
       else: ...
    if exact_interval != float('inf'):
       exact_seq, exact_score = _exact_update(best, exact_seq, exact_score)
    return best, exact_seq, exact_score
```

```python
"""
Search non-negative sequences minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / (sum(a))^2
for the provided evaluator.

The search uses:
  - FFT evaluation of the full self-convolution,
  - spectral box-projection initializations that force the convolution
    toward a flat profile,
  - Frank-Wolfe / pairwise / log-domain local polishing,
  - optional semidefinite-free SCA-style LP refinements when SciPy is available.
"""

import time
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog as _linprog
    _HAS_LINPROG = True
except Exception:
    _linprog = None
    _HAS_LINPROG = False

_TARGET_SCORE = 1.5027


def _next_pow2(m):
    p = 1
    while p < m:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def _make_ctx(n):
    b_len = 2 * n - 1
    Lb = _next_pow2(b_len)
    Lg = _next_pow2(b_len + n - 1)
    offset = Lg - b_len
    idx = Lg - 1 - np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
    return (n, Lb, Lg, offset, idx, b_len)


def _normalize_nonnegative(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.ndim != 1:
        x = x.ravel()
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
    s = float(x.sum())
    if x.size == 0 or not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        if x.size == 0:
            return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
        return np.full(x.size, 1.0 / x.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return x / s


def _resample_sequence(seq, n):
    arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = _normalize_nonnegative(arr)
    if arr.size == n:
        return arr
    if arr.size == 1:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, arr.size - 1.0, arr.size)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, arr.size - 1.0, n)
    y = np.interp(x_new, x_old, arr)
    y = np.maximum(y, 0.0)
    s = float(y.sum())
    if s > 1e-300:
        y /= s
    else:
        y = np.full(n, 1.0 / n)
    return y


def _fft_conv(a, L):
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    return np.fft.irfft(A * A, L)


def _score_ctx(a, ctx):
    n, Lb, Lg, offset, idx, b_len = ctx
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-300:
        return float("inf")
    b = _fft_conv(a, Lb)
    b = b[:b_len]
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    if b.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    mb = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(mb):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * mb / (S * S))


def _softmax(theta):
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64)
    if theta.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    z = theta - float(np.max(theta))
    a = np.exp(z)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(theta.size, 1.0 / theta.size)
    return a / s


def _correlate_valid(a, q, ctx):
    n, Lb, Lg, offset, idx, b_len = ctx
    revq = np.zeros(Lg, dtype=np.float64)
    revq[offset:offset + q.size] = q[::-1]
    C = np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(a, Lg) * np.fft.rfft(revq, Lg), Lg)
    return C[idx]


def _make_q(b, mb, sharpness):
    if b.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if mb <= 0.0 or sharpness <= 0.0:
        return np.full(b.size, 1.0 / b.size)
    tau = float(mb) / float(sharpness)
    x = (b - mb) / tau
    x = x - float(np.max(x))
    q = np.exp(x)
    s = float(q.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(b.size, 1.0 / b.size)
    return q / s


def _project_box(b, T):
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    m = b.size
    if m == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    s = float(b.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(m, 1.0 / m)
    b = b / s
    avg = 1.0 / m
    if T < avg:
        T = avg
    if float(np.max(b)) <= T:
        ratio = T / avg
        if ratio < 10.0:
            w = max(0.0, min(1.0, (10.0 - ratio) / 9.0))
            b = (1.0 - w) * b + w * avg
            b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
            sb = float(b.sum())
            if sb > 1e-300:
                b /= sb
        return b
    bcap = np.minimum(b, T)
    deficit = 1.0 - float(bcap.sum())
    if deficit <= 1e-12:
        return bcap
    mask = bcap < T
    if not np.any(mask):
        return bcap
    space = T - bcap[mask]
    cap_space = float(space.sum())
    if cap_space <= 1e-300:
        return bcap
    if deficit > cap_space:
        bcap = np.full(m, T, dtype=np.float64)
        bcap /= bcap.sum()
        return bcap
    bcap[mask] += deficit * space / cap_space
    bcap = np.maximum(bcap, 0.0)
    sb = float(bcap.sum())
    if sb > 1e-300:
        bcap /= sb
    return bcap


def _spectral_project(a, btarget, A, L, rng, perturb=0.01):
    B = np.fft.rfft(btarget, L)
    nyq_abs = 0.0
    if L % 2 == 0:
        nyq_abs = abs(float(np.real(B[L // 2])))
        B = B.copy()
        B[L // 2] = nyq_abs + 0j
    A2 = np.sqrt(B + 0j)

    if A is not None and A.size == A2.size:
        dot = A2 * np.conj(A)
        mask = np.real(dot) < 0.0
        if np.any(mask):
            A2[mask] *= -1.0

    if rng is not None and rng.random() < perturb:
        flip = rng.random(A2.size) < 0.01
        if np.any(flip):
            A2[flip] *= -1.0

    if L % 2 == 0:
        root = float(np.sqrt(nyq_abs))
        if A is not None and A.size > L // 2:
            prev = float(np.real(A[L // 2]))
            if root * prev < 0.0:
                root = -root
        A2[L // 2] = root + 0j

    a_new = np.fft.irfft(A2, L)[:a.size]
    a_new = np.clip(a_new, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a_new.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return a, A
    a_new /= s
    A_new = np.fft.rfft(a_new, L)
    return a_new, A_new


def _spectral_reach(a, ctx, time_limit, rng, target_score=1.51):
    n, Lb, Lg, offset, idx, b_len = ctx
    a = _normalize_nonnegative(a)
    if a.size == 0 or time_limit <= 0.0:
        return a, _score_ctx(a, ctx)
    t_end = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(time_limit))
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, Lb)
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = _score_ctx(a, ctx)
    current_score = best_score

    T = max(best_score / (2.0 * n), 1e-300)
    avg = 1.0 / b_len
    T_floor = max(avg * (1.0 + 1e-6), target_score / (2.0 * n))
    if T <= T_floor:
        return best, best_score

    outer = 0
    while time.monotonic() < t_end and T > T_floor and outer < 20000:
        T = max(T_floor, T * 0.99)
        for _ in range(8):
            if time.monotonic() >= t_end:
                break
            b = _fft_conv(a, Lb)[:b_len]
            b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
            s = float(b.sum())
            if s > 1e-300:
                b = b / s
            else:
                b = np.full(b_len, avg)
            btarget = _project_box(b, T)

            if rng is not None and rng.random() < 0.18:
                noise = rng.normal(0.0, max(1e-15, 0.06 * T), b_len)
                btarget = btarget + noise
                btarget = np.maximum(btarget, 0.0)
                nb = float(btarget.sum())
                if nb > 1e-300:
                    btarget = btarget / nb

            a, A = _spectral_project(a, btarget, A, Lb, rng, perturb=0.012)
            sc = _score_ctx(a, ctx)
            current_score = sc
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best = a.copy()
                best_score = sc

        if current_score > best_score * 2.0 and current_score > best_score + 1e-4:
            a = best.copy()
            A = np.fft.rfft(a, Lb)
            current_score = best_score

        if best_score < T * (2.0 * n):
            T = max(T_floor, best_score / (2.0 * n))
        outer += 1

    return best, best_score


def _dirichlet_start(n, alpha, rng):
    if n <= 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    x = rng.gamma(alpha, 1.0, size=n)
    s = float(x.sum())
    if np.isfinite(s) and s > 1e-300:
        return x / s
    return np.full(n, 1.0 / n)


def _exp_center_start(n, rng):
    if n <= 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    p = int(rng.integers(0, n))
    beta = float(rng.uniform(0.25, 5.0))
    x = np.exp(-beta * np.abs(np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) - p))
    s = float(x.sum())
    if np.isfinite(s) and s > 1e-300:
        return x / s
    return np.full(n, 1.0 / n)


def _random_perturb(a, sigma, rng):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    y = a * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, a.size))
    return _normalize_nonnegative(y)


def _fw_candidates(g, rng, n, bcand=8, rcand=4):
    if n <= 0:
        return []
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
        return [int(rng.integers(0, n))]
    if n <= bcand + rcand:
        return np.arange(n).tolist()
    bc = max(1, min(int(bcand), n - 1))
    low = np.argpartition(g, bc - 1)[:bc]
    rand = rng.integers(0, n, size=rcand)
    extra = np.array([0, n - 1], dtype=np.int64)
    cands = np.unique(np.concatenate((low, rand, extra)))
    return [int(x) for x in cands]


def _try_vertex(a, j, score, ctx, t_values):
    for t in t_values:
        at = a * (1.0 - t)
        at[j] += t
        s = float(at.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300:
            continue
        at = at / s
        sc = _score_ctx(at, ctx)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < score - 1e-12:
            return at, sc
    return None


def _try_pair(a, i, j, score, ctx, fracs):
    if i == j:
        return None
    for f in fracs:
        delta = f * float(a[i])
        if delta <= 0.0:
            continue
        if delta > float(a[i]):
            delta = float(a[i])
        at = a.copy()
        at[i] -= delta
        at[j] += delta
        s = float(at.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300:
            continue
        at = at / s
        sc = _score_ctx(at, ctx)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < score - 1e-12:
            return at, sc
    return None


def _try_log_step(a, g, score, ctx, alphas):
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    mean = float(np.dot(a, g))
    h = a * (g - mean)
    maxh = float(np.max(np.abs(h)))
    if not np.isfinite(maxh) or maxh <= 1e-300:
        return None
    hn = h / maxh
    theta = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    for alpha in alphas:
        theta_try = theta - alpha * hn
        theta_try -= float(np.mean(theta_try))
        at = _softmax(theta_try)
        sc = _score_ctx(at, ctx)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < score - 1e-12:
            return at, sc
    return None


def _exact_update(a, best_seq, best_score):
    if a is None:
        return best_seq, best_score
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return best_seq, best_score
    if arr.size == 0:
        return best_seq, best_score
    arr = _normalize_nonnegative(arr)
    seq = [float(x) for x in arr]
    try:
        sc = float(evaluate_sequence(seq))
    except Exception:
        sc = float("inf")
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
        return seq, sc
    return best_seq, best_score


def _fw_optimize(a, ctx, time_limit, rng):
    n, Lb, Lg, offset, idx, b_len = ctx
    a = _normalize_nonnegative(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        empty_exact, empty_exact_score = _exact_update([], [], float("inf"))
        return np.ones(1), empty_exact, empty_exact_score

    best = a.copy()
    best_score = _score_ctx(a, ctx)
    exact_seq, exact_score = [], float("inf")
    last_exact = time.monotonic()

    if time_limit <= 0.0:
        exact_seq, exact_score = _exact_update(best, exact_seq, exact_score)
        return best, exact_seq, exact_score

    t_end = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(time_limit))
    sharpness = 40.0
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0

    if n <= 4096:
        exact_interval = 5.0
    elif n <= 8192:
        exact_interval = 10.0
    else:
        exact_interval = float("inf")

    vertex_ts = (0.08, 0.03, 0.01, 0.003)
    pair_fs = (0.35, 0.12, 0.04, 0.01)
    log_alphas = (0.40, 0.15, 0.05, 0.015)

    while time.monotonic() < t_end:
        b = _fft_conv(a, Lb)[:b_len]
        b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
        S = float(a.sum())
        if S <= 1e-300:
            a = best.copy()
            continue
        mb = float(np.max(b))
        score = float(2.0 * n * mb / (S * S))
        if not np.isfinite(score):
            a = best.copy()
            continue

        if score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best = a.copy()
            best_score = score
            if exact_interval != float("inf") and time.monotonic() - last_exact > 0.5:
                exact_seq, exact_score = _exact_update(a, exact_seq, exact_score)
                last_exact = time.monotonic()
        elif exact_interval != float("inf") and time.monotonic() - last_exact > exact_interval:
            exact_seq, exact_score = _exact_update(best, exact_seq, exact_score)
            last_exact = time.monotonic()

        q = _make_q(b, mb, sharpness)
        c = _correlate_valid(a, q, ctx)
        g = c
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
            a = _random_perturb(best, 0.07, rng)
            sharpness = 40.0
            no_improve = 0
            continue

        accepted = False

        candidates = _fw_candidates(g, rng, n, bcand=8 if n > 5000 else 12, rcand=4)
        for j in candidates:
            res = _try_vertex(a, int(j), score, ctx, vertex_ts)
            if res is not None:
                a, score = res
                accepted = True
                break

        if not accepted and n > 12:
            k = min(5, n)
            low = np.argpartition(g, k - 1)[:k]
            high = np.argpartition(-g, k - 1)[:k]
            for hi in high[:3]:
                for lo in low[:3]:
                    res = _try_pair(a, int(hi), int(lo), score, ctx, pair_fs)
                    if res is not None:
                        a, score = res
                        accepted = True
                        break
                if accepted:
                    break

        if not accepted:
            res = _try_log_step(a, g, score, ctx, log_alphas)
            if res is not None:
                a, score = res
                accepted = True

        if accepted:
            no_improve = 0
            it += 1
            if it % 12 == 0 and sharpness < 600.0:
                sharpness *= 1.06
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve == 25:
                sharpness = min(1200.0, sharpness * 1.25)
            if no_improve % 8 == 0:
                ap = _random_perturb(a, 0.05, rng)
                sc = _score_ctx(ap, ctx)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < score:
                    a = ap
            if no_improve > 50:
                rand = _dirichlet_start(n, 1.0, rng)
                a = 0.75 * best + 0.25 * rand
                a = _normalize_nonnegative(a)
                sharpness = 40.0
                no_improve = 0

    if exact_interval != float("inf"):
        exact_seq, exact_score = _exact_update(best, exact_seq, exact_score)
    return best, exact_seq, exact_score


def _lp_polish(a, ctx, time_limit, rng):
    if not _HAS_LINPROG or time_limit <= 0.0:
        return a
    n, Lb, Lg, offset, idx, b_len = ctx
    a = _normalize_nonnegative(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    if n > 16000:
        return a

    best = a.copy()
    best_score = _score_ctx(a, ctx)
    t_end = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(time_limit))
    K = 120 if n > 5000 else 200
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < t_end and it < 800:
        b = _fft_conv(a, Lb)[:b_len]
        b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
        s = float(b.sum())
        if s > 1e-300:
            b = b / s
        M = float(np.max(b))
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break

        top_size = min(2 * K, b_len)
        if top_size < b_len:
            top = np.argpartition(b, top_size - 1)[-top_size:]
            active = rng.choice(top, K, replace=False)
        else:
            active = np.arange(b_len)
        active = np.asarray(active, dtype=np.int64)

        Arows = np.empty((K, n), dtype=np.float64)
        bvals = np.empty(K, dtype=np.float64)
        for r, k in enumerate(active):
            k = int(k)
            bvals[r] = b[k]
            lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
            hi = min(n - 1, k)
            Arows[r, lo:hi + 1] = a[k - hi:k - lo + 1][::-1]

        c = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
        c[-1] = 1.0

        A_ub = np.empty((K, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
        A_ub[:, :n] = 2.0 * Arows
        A_ub[:, -1] = -1.0

        A_eq = np.ones((1, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
        A_eq[0, -1] = 0.0
        b_eq = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)

        ymax = max(0.05, 1.5 * float(np.max(a)))
        ymax = min(0.5, ymax)
        bounds = [(0.0, ymax)] * n + [(0.0, None)]

        try:
            res = _linprog(
                c,
                A_ub=A_ub,
                b_ub=bvals,
                A_eq=A_eq,
                b_eq=b_eq,
                bounds=bounds,
                method="highs",
            )
        except Exception:
            res = None

        accepted = False
        if res is not None and getattr(res, "success", False):
            y = np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=np.float64)
            y = _normalize_nonnegative(y)
            for lam in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625, 0.03125):
                at = (1.0 - lam) * a + lam * y
                at = _normalize_nonnegative(at)
                sc = _score_ctx(at, ctx)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                    a = at
                    best = at.copy()
                    best_score = sc
                    accepted = True
                    break

        if not accepted and it % 5 == 0:
            ap = _random_perturb(a, 0.05, rng)
            sc = _score_ctx(ap, ctx)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                a = ap
                best = ap.copy()
                best_score = sc

        it += 1

    return best


def _optimize_start(a, ctx, budget, rng, do_lp=True):
    a = _normalize_nonnegative(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        empty_exact, empty_exact_score = _exact_update([], [], float("inf"))
        return np.ones(1), empty_exact, empty_exact_score

    t_end = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(budget))
    sc = _score_ctx(a, ctx)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        a = np.full(a.size, 1.0 / a.size)
        sc = _score_ctx(a, ctx)

    if sc > 1.56 and (t_end - time.monotonic()) > 25.0:
        spec_budget = min(90.0, (t_end - time.monotonic()) * 0.35)
        if spec_budget > 8.0:
            a, sc = _spectral_reach(a, ctx, spec_budget, rng, target_score=1.51)

    fw_budget = max(0.0, t_end - time.monotonic())
    use_lp = do_lp and _HAS_LINPROG and a.size <= 12000
    if use_lp:
        reserved = min(70.0, fw_budget * 0.25)
        fw_budget = max(0.0, fw_budget - reserved)

    a_int, fw_exact_seq, fw_exact_score = _fw_optimize(a, ctx, fw_budget, rng)

    sc_int = _score_ctx(a_int, ctx)
    if use_lp and np.isfinite(sc_int) and sc_int < 1.72:
        rem = max(0.0, t_end - time.monotonic())
        if rem > 12.0:
            a_lp = _lp_polish(a_int, ctx, min(rem, 70.0), rng)
            sc_lp = _score_ctx(a_lp, ctx)
            if np.isfinite(sc_lp) and sc_lp < sc_int:
                a_int = a_lp
                sc_int = sc_lp

    exact_seq, exact_score = fw_exact_seq, fw_exact_score
    exact_seq, exact_score = _exact_update(a_int, exact_seq, exact_score)
    return a_int, exact_seq, exact_score


def _search_n(n, budget, rng, base_arr, best_seq, best_score):
    if n <= 0 or budget <= 0.0:
        return best_seq, best_score
    t_end = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(budget))
    ctx = _make_ctx(int(n))

    starts = []
    starts.append(("uniform", np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)))

    if best_seq is not None and len(best_seq) > 0:
        best_start = _resample_sequence(best_seq, n)
        starts.append(("best", _random_perturb(best_start, 0.08, rng)))

    if base_arr is not None:
        base_start = _resample_sequence(base_arr, n)
        starts.append(("base", base_start))
        starts.append(("base-pert", _random_perturb(base_start, 0.25, rng)))

    starts.append(("dirichlet", _dirichlet_start(n, 1.0, rng)))
    starts.append(("exp", _exp_center_start(n, rng)))
    starts.append(("sparse", _dirichlet_start(n, 0.35, rng)))

    rem0 = max(0.0, t_end - time.monotonic())
    if rem0 < 160:
        max_runs = 1
    elif rem0 < 420:
        max_runs = 2
    elif rem0 < 900:
        max_runs = 4
    else:
        max_runs = len(starts)
    starts = starts[:max_runs]

    for idx, (name, a0) in enumerate(starts):
        rem = t_end - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 8.0:
            break
        remaining = len(starts) - idx
        if remaining == 1:
            budget_run = rem
        else:
            if idx == 0:
                budget_run = min(rem * 0.45, max(rem / max(1, remaining), 60.0))
            else:
                budget_run = rem / float(remaining)
        if budget_run < 6.0:
            budget_run = rem

        print(
            f"search n={n} start {idx} ({name}) budget={budget_run:.1f}s "
            f"current_best={best_score:.6f}",
            flush=True,
        )

        a_opt, ex_seq, ex_score = _optimize_start(
            a0, ctx, budget_run, rng, do_lp=(n <= 12000 and _HAS_LINPROG)
        )

        if ex_seq is not None and len(ex_seq) > 0 and np.isfinite(ex_score):
            if ex_score < best_score:
                best_seq = list(ex_seq)
                best_score = float(ex_score)
        else:
            best_seq, best_score = _exact_update(a_opt, best_seq, best_score)

        print(
            f"search n={n} start {idx} done best={best_score:.6f} "
            f"time_left={max(0.0, t_end - time.monotonic()):.1f}s",
            flush=True,
        )

        if np.isfinite(best_score) and best_score < _TARGET_SCORE - 0.005:
            break

    return best_seq, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    """
    Main entry point.  Searches several lengths and randomized starting
    sequences, then returns the best exact-evaluated sequence found.
    """
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t_start + max(0.0, float(budget_s) - 5.0)
    total_budget = max(0.0, deadline - t_start)

    best_seq = []
    best_score = float("inf")

    base_raw = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    base_arr = None
    if base_raw is not None:
        try:
            base_arr = np.asarray(base_raw, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            base_arr = np.where(np.isfinite(base_arr), base_arr, 0.0)
            s = float(base_arr.sum())
            if np.isfinite(s) and s > 1e-300:
                base_arr = base_arr / s
            else:
                base_arr = None
        except Exception:
            base_arr = None

    n1 = 4096
    if base_arr is not None and 3000 <= base_arr.size <= 5100:
        n1 = int(base_arr.size)

    n2 = 8192 if n1 != 8192 else 4096

    if total_budget < 600.0:
        plan = [(n1, 1.0)]
    elif total_budget < 1100.0:
        plan = [(n1, 0.65), (n2, 0.35)]
    else:
        plan = [(n1, 0.45), (n2, 0.30), (16384, 0.25)]

    for n, frac in plan:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        alloc = min(max(0.0, rem), total_budget * max(0.0, float(frac)))
        if alloc < 25.0:
            continue
        print(f"planning search n={n} alloc={alloc:.1f}s total_budget={total_budget:.1f}s", flush=True)
        best_seq, best_score = _search_n(int(n), alloc, rng, base_arr, best_seq, best_score)
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break

    if not best_seq:
        nf = int(n1)
        best_seq = [1.0 / nf] * nf
        try:
            _ = evaluate_sequence(best_seq)
        except Exception:
            pass
    else:
        try:
            arr = _normalize_nonnegative(best_seq)
            best_seq = [float(x) for x in arr]
        except Exception:
            pass

    print(f"final exact best={best_score:.6f} len={len(best_seq)}", flush=True)
    return best_seq
```
